<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>A $975 million deal will turn this Khaby Lame into an </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5554c588-0a3d-4553-9936-0c46a3fb5fdc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI‑powered, multilingual content machine</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Trader Joe’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/75755280-5f0e-4c66-bbce-1732267893f5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">surpasses</a><span> Publix in customer satisfaction ranking.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cd0f42df-a295-473a-90a0-5cd55f1ca28c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Enshittifinancial</a><span> Crisis.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Brands pull plug on </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0bb6105a-3c63-4c0d-841d-adec9628497e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Cha Eun-woo ads</a><span> as criminal evasion team digs into tax-dodging allegations.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The one goal that </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/29475665-543a-49d6-863a-c2f64c04975c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">unites</a><span> most Gen Z men.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Actor Tom Holland’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d831d279-53d3-4dcd-b40a-c0b7eda9c308?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Nonalcoholic Beer BERO</a><span> gets private-equity backing.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Zohran Mamdani wore </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0de9ca5c-6e02-41af-9229-ff671efed37b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">custom Carhartt</a><span> to tackle his first snowstorm as mayor.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<h3 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.375em;">Shall we have the talk? Brands, Culture &amp; Fashion…</h3>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">The vibes of a very existing fashion week were offset by various figures and mostly announcements. (All links to campaigns/news are in other sections below.) </p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>First, you have déjà vu. Some people</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/34f41290-5267-4409-85b6-d024a40e9204?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> forgot the racist history</a><span> of Dolce and Gabbana and were outraged by the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/97954440-8789-4f48-9000-370046f37997?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">all-white casting</a><span> of their recent show. The reactions varied from f\*\*k them, to who cares, to what’s the IG handle of that model? There are enough things to be outraged about these days. It’s better to not give D&amp;G any coverage. Silence is the best answer to people who don’t believe in accountability.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Second, the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f54a7424-c1c8-4c4f-8bfb-a417746eed2b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">sighting of Lauren Sanchez</a><span> with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8f480fc2-c42c-470d-a4e9-2e5056f54951?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Anna Wintour</a><span> almost gave fashionistas </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9f1e8d42-e8ea-4fbd-9bfb-f8460be39600?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a heart attack</a><span>, a totally reasonable response. It also came to light that the CFDA Fashion Awards are sponsored by Amazon Fashion. And last, more people became aware of Law Roach’s role as Lauren Sanchez’s stylist. Did it break hearts? </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a371b19e-56a9-4f38-831e-da1b122b8001?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Idk</a><span>.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">On the runway, Jonathan Anderson’s men’s collection created a debate around following the brand principles vs. providing a provocative critique on the existing fashion codes and general trends. I personally liked the collection. It’s creating a meaningful dialogue. Now, I can’t say the same about Prada. The collection has symmetry and the messaging is clear, but it’s a critique that takes itself too seriously. Meanwhile, Willy Chavarria brought the culture to the stage. Instead of a critique, it asked you to see more than fashion, and Valentino Couture said you only get to see the fashion.</p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">To sum up, a good fashion week for the major brands trying to tell a story. Dior and Prada Menswear became a spectrum of what critique means, meanwhile Chavarria and Valentino said zoom in, zoom out.</p></blockquote>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>The worst and the best off the runway. Lyas throwing </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1ad76bd3-b436-436a-bae3-95f32e32112b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">watch parties</a><span> and bringing people closer to fashion was the best, and Nothing joining the party to give away free headphones was strategic and a nice gesture. Meanwhile, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/795cc35b-cccf-41e1-a79e-09f7d7d7e182?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Alexis Mabille</a><span> creating an AI slop couture show was the worst for obvious reasons.</span></p>
<h4 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.125em;">Super Bowl LX</h4>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">I am exhausted by the teasers and random cryptic posts being made by brands ahead of the Super Bowl. I am calling it the Christmasization of the Super Bowl. Soon you will have brands launching commercials not one week before, it will be three weeks of teasing the collab, and then still launching the ad before the big game. I know, I am a marketer, I shouldn’t be the one complaining, so let’s get to the ads.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>The most popular SB ad so far is </span><em>Kendall Jenner’s Fanatics Sportsbook</em><span>, and yeah, the ad is good, but way too many people are overlooking the fact that it promotes gambling. We are back to the 60s. Gambling ads are the new cigarette ads. The ads are too good for some people to complain, and gambling platforms are everywhere, so you can’t complain. The second and third places are being held by </span><em>Budweiser</em><span> and </span><em>Pepsi</em><span>. One is banking on Americana, the other on good ol’ brand rivalry.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">The general theme of this year’s ads is to either embrace the cringe or embrace someone cringe. You have Benson Boone, Adrien Brody, Tim Robinson, Bradley Cooper, Guy Fieri, and more celebs who fall into one of the two categories.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">CeraVe went with their once creative, now default playbook of using a social media moment as the key plot of the Super Bowl ad. They are doing the ashy ankles spot with Kevin Durant. It’s not bad, but the best use of social media winner this year has to be either Coors Light or...</p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="695"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8b45863e-e4d2-4009-a764-9d5f5682c162?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="Willy Chavarria Fall-Winter 2026 Captures the Zeitgeist - Sharp Magazine" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/ffe517b7-f40e-40e4-ac77-862fef460264\_1920x1200.jpeg","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":910,"width":1456,"resizeWidth":695,"bytes":null,"alt":"Willy Chavarria Fall-Winter 2026 Captures the Zeitgeist - Sharp Magazine","title":null,"type":null,"href":null,"belowTheFold":true,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":null,"isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="343.75" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!jmrc!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fffe517b7-f40e-40e4-ac77-862fef460264\_1920x1200.jpeg" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" title="Willy Chavarria Fall-Winter 2026 Captures the Zeitgeist - Sharp Magazine" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table>
<figcaption style="box-sizing: content-box;color: rgb(119,119,119);font-size: 14px;line-height: 20px;font-weight: 400;letter-spacing: -.15px;margin-top: 8px;width: 70%;padding-left: 15%;padding-right: 15%;text-align: center;">Willy Chavarria Fall-Winter 2026 </figcaption></figure></div>
<h4 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.125em;">AI-Slop Marketing War 2.0</h4>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>I </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/eddc6087-5e92-4bcb-aab4-cfffc9b65250?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">reported</a><span> last month that brands in Switzerland normalized AI slop with the “Grittibenz” trend, which was partially human, but mostly AI. We have officially entered stage two of this slop war, with the</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0be466fe-79b1-46f6-93e7-3a10c4b872a2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> nihilist penguin</a><span> becoming the new warrior for brands. First, brands in the U.S. possibly backed out of this trend after seeing the White House post the AI version of this meme. That wasn’t the case for brands in Germany. </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ef53c0d8-bcb9-4c2d-b67a-e9755489f404?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Lidl’s AI slop version</a><span> of the meme went viral, and most German brands </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d060df3b-7354-472a-8f1e-14e3bb1dca1b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">started</a><span> engaging with the trend. The penguin became a temporary symbol of brand stans, if we are taking their word for it. As far as I know, the first big entry from the UK was </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e1a3e216-2ea9-430b-95cc-5c4fb38cb87f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Tesco</a><span> creating an AI slop version of the meme, identical to Lidl.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>The situation isn’t all so nihilistic, with every brand using </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bea64f2d-78fb-4b9e-b2bf-d4cf764ad087?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI</a><span>. Some opted for human creativity. </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/77340f5d-b239-4868-9206-45d4feaea106?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Google India</a><span data-component-name="FootnoteAnchorToDOMStatic" style="font-size: 20px;">¹</span><span> didn’t even show the penguin. </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0bdff6e6-ce55-4a3d-9df3-2066f39d0622?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">BMW</a><span> used an overlay of the original video. </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f3910e0c-a66d-4561-92ff-306458bca1f9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">IKEA Switzerland</a><span> possibly licensed an image. Currys had a human wear a penguin costume. Audi </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3da5e91d-637e-4b66-8806-c573e9c02a8c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">went</a><span> simple, and maybe there are a few other brands joining this trend without using AI slop. Btw, you have Lidl DE (who did use AI) ragebaiting </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/228d9db5-f1f5-49c4-8453-64d9e90a83bd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">under a post that clearly doesn’t use AI</a><span> with the comment, “is this AI?”</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>As usual, I would like to remind you that AI slop and AI content are two different things. There is an AI agency called “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a64bd424-682b-47e4-8a3b-8e2922c7d965?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Studio Misoo</a><span>,” and they are doing content around brand collaborations, imagining them through AI tools. Their work looks very real and isn’t destroying something original, to say the least. Meanwhile, you can always tell that brands didn’t make any effort to share a story in trends like the one above</span><em><strong>. Lastly, most trends in general are meaningless slop. </strong></em><span>Add AI on top of it, and you get nothing but engagement that likely means nothing.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">On the other side of the world, I have a an assumption that two campaigns that happened this week, one from McDonald’s Qatar and the other from Heinz UAE were AI-Assisted in some way. And they have useful insights behind them, and it’s not pure slop, as it’s not that easy to tell if they are AI-generated. Both Heinz and McDonald’s are known for experimental campaigns that usually include zoomed in visuals and have design traits that AI is really good at generating, so it’s going to be hard to tell when AI was used or not. Last, something else informing my assumptions is I’ve seen a big push from MENA agencies for AI-generated campaigns. </p>
<h4 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.125em;">Brands vs. Politics</h4>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Many brands are now participating in economic boycotts, making posts against ICE, and donating to NGOs and immigration protection programs. But as usual, there is a spectrum of beliefs and actions. FIGS </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6911c1ae-5b91-4b19-a723-e0f1a02d67e0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">donated $25k</a><span> to Alex Pretti’s GoFundMe page and </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9fb37f66-9de9-4426-903b-af56e506bf1d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">posted</a><span> about t</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5b8189da-7673-4b4c-a4f3-3da52fc3bf4c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">he murder of Pretti</a><span> on their Instagram. Meanwhile, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/59fd1fbe-3daa-44a9-8262-958e95e5efb9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Hootsuite</a><span> was called out for working with ICE, and the apology letter wasn’t embraced. It lacked emotion, and many </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f43cf834-4197-492d-8b5e-a34be56805c4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">bashed it as an AI-generated apology</a><span> with no respect for the lives that are impacted by the actions of ICE. That’s the story of two brands. What about the others?</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>LinkedIn is getting filled with advice on “Should brands engage with politics or stay out of the loop?” and the answers vary from No to Yes to Mark Ritson. There are new levels to being apolitical and performative. Mark posted</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/344a1b0e-7e28-495c-b522-1ceef3cff0bf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> two opinion pieces</a><span> on Adweek, one saying brands shouldn’t talk about politics, the other partially advocating for it. Is it him being objective? I don’t think so. Objectivity gives you a clear answer, it doesn’t confuse you into oblivion. </span><span data-component-name="MentionStatic" style="white-space: nowrap;cursor: pointer;color: #e85c57;border-radius: 4px;transition: color .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),background-color .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),box-shadow .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),border .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),border-radius .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),opacity .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),filter .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),stroke .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),transform .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),scale .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1),outline .25scubic-bezier(.19,1,.22,1);margin: 0 -.125em;padding: 0 .125em .0625em;text-decoration: none;"><a href="https://open.substack.com/users/102805934-ashley-rutstein?utm\_source=mentions" style="-webkit-text-decoration: none;color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Ashley Rutstein</a></span><span> from Stuff About Advertising has a better and clearer answer for brands:</span></p>
<div data-component-name="EmbeddedPostToDOMStatic" style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;text-align: center;direction: ltr;"><div style="font-size: 16px;display: block;text-decoration: none;font-family: system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';color: #363737;line-height: 1.5;text-align: left;max-width: 600px;box-sizing: border-box;border-radius: 12px;border: 1px solid rgb(0,0,0,.1);margin: 36px auto;padding: 20px;">
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;height: 28px;display: flex;align-items: center;justify-content: flex-start;padding-bottom: 20px;border-bottom: 1px solid rgb(0,0,0,.1);margin-bottom: 20px;">
<img height="28" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!q2to!,w\_56,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F46b3dfdf-8916-40e6-b6e1-29903316c87e\_500x500.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;max-width: 100%;height: 28px;margin: 0 12px 0 0;width: 28px;border-radius: 8px;" width="28"/><span style="font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: 500;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;font-size: 16px;line-height: 28px;">Stuff About Advertising</span>
</div>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 8px;display: flex;align-items: flex-start;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/68c9c9c5-1cfb-4e65-90e8-bdce84281fc9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: 700;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;color: #363737;font-size: 18px;text-decoration: none;">Should brands speak up about politics?</a></div>
<div style="font-family: system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-size: 16px;line-height: 1.6;margin-bottom: 16px;color: #777777;margin-top: 4px;">I considered not sending a newsletter this week…</div>
<div style="display: inline-flex;box-sizing: border-box;border-radius: 8px;border: 1px solid rgb(0,0,0,.1);padding: 10px 12px;font-size: 16px;line-height: 16px;margin-bottom: 16px;font-weight: 500;align-items: center;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/68c9c9c5-1cfb-4e65-90e8-bdce84281fc9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #363737;text-decoration: none;">Read more</a></div>
<div style="line-height: 26px;color: #777777;font-size: 14px;">3 days ago · 5 likes · 1 comment · Ashley Rutstein</div>
</div></div>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Having talked a lot about </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/20e92977-631b-448a-9ceb-f5448d1bf5cd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">brand purpose</a><span> in the past, my first answer is “</span><em>Know yourself and know your customer</em><span>.” And more importantly, remember doing something on-ground is more important than posting on social.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><strong>TL;DR answer</strong><span>: If you think your post can make people take action, do it. If you think your post is only going to divide the comments and create no action, put the boots on the ground and take an action that helps people around you. If you are thinking about a both-sides/apolitical post, don’t make a post. If you haven’t spoken about any other political issues so far and plan to post something now, it’s still worth taking that action, just prepare yourself to ignore the whataboutism.</span></p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">The ifs and buts are many, the answers may vary, but the objective is the same, it needs to create real change, not fake engagement.</p></blockquote>
<h4 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.125em;">Marketing Trends from campaigns that happened this week</h4>
<ol style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>First, Valentine’s Day campaigns are being overshadowed by all the noise from the Super Bowl, the Olympics, and a few other campaigns. The focus so far in VD campaigns has been on hands and products. The brands winning the day are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b0f5b410-52f6-4564-bc12-ce5775e20a05?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">UGG</a><span>, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c5467ce0-5be3-448e-8f1e-fc2a8bfbd6c9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Cartier</a><span>, and </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/85686cde-104f-4f3f-b8c8-170596d9159c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Havaianas</a><span>. Are duos also a symbol of holding hands? Chanel’s Valentine’s campaign </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/384d9b2a-54f8-4278-b23f-dd7bd9239367?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">is</a><span> about </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/94211242-66cd-4bf3-a6d5-c5a7de0e39a5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">duos</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Last year, multiple Substack articles were pushing for brands to save the print media and magazine culture. Similarly, predictions about brands becoming media brands were all over the place. Now that you have a </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c847fcb9-d4ed-442a-8028-239171954dc0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Chief Entertainment Officer</a><span> at GAP and MAC Cosmetics relaunching their magazine, will they save the media ecosystem?</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>The </span><em><span>Romeo x Dr. Pepper jingle ad</span><strong> </strong></em><span>wasn’t just good for one brand, it ended up giving tons of free exposure to almost hundreds of brands. As far as I know, the amount Dr. Pepper paid to Romeo for that jingle remains anonymous, but rumors of her getting a two million dollar check started going viral, which led to a ton of other people trying to create the next viral jingle. And some videos went viral, some didn’t. Brands commented under the videos for free exposure, but no deals. What’s the lesson here? Well, there isn’t one, it’s just the way social media works. People always try to replicate the success of other people on the internet. Well, that is a lesson, maybe brands should factor how much X post will make other people and brands likely to recreate it in their own way.</span></p>
<ol style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;list-style-type: lower-alpha;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>For my chronically online people, the drama </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3bcefd3b-c870-45b2-b3d8-3e4dcca228cc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">reminded me</a><span> of the Brooke Monk vs. Natalie Reynolds situation.</span></p></li></ol>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>OOH marketing continues to be more and more about interactions, attention is a side hustle now. We’ve </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e03bfb13-b838-4aa5-8175-8734d2b58f08?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Sony PlayStation’s playable billboard</a><span> vs. FCA’s scam awareness billboard. Both could be classified as limited activations, but I guess marketing them as billboards seems more innovative. And that’s the story, billboards are being gamified to generate interactions over attention, because real attention is on social?</span></p>
<ol style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;list-style-type: lower-alpha;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">Well, my theory says most people who would’ve seen Stella Artois’ snow billboard might’ve said, oh, it’s just snow from the winter storm on top of the billboard. Meanwhile, the social audiences were made aware of the fact that the snow filled the oversized glass and created an illusion of beer foam. That context matters, and that’s how experimental OOH ads get a new life on social. </p></li></ol>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Oreo continued the trend of companies finding their brand assets in the wild. The brand showed real cows that naturally resemble the iconic cookie in their new campaign. This campaign made me recall the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/50b0773f-2379-4aa9-97c4-e62f7dc41e00?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">controversial Mercedes Benz campaign</a><span> that highlighted the existence of their logo in nature, and the “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f378019c-6951-4f39-95dd-6ba09c44f448?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">better with pepsi</a><span>” campaign, which was also logo-driven. Lol, how can I forget the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/db286853-fac1-432f-8190-a769cd9b28d9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">MTA x Nathan’s famous campaign</a><span> I covered last year?</span><em> I am a sucker for these campaigns, it shows that brands pay attention to the random things around them to find something unique.</em></p>
<ol style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;list-style-type: lower-alpha;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">The Babybel wax vinyls collaboration with Timbaland is a similar example of brands finding an idea in detail. It was literally hidden in the product.</p></li></ol>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>To celebrate the 20th anniversary of </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b470c85c-9dca-4f43-8b12-88c7da87d1d5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">High School Musical</a><span>, Disney </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4ee9fc00-c376-4021-a6ab-c4d7795f2b91?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">released</a><span> the whole movie in 52 parts on TikTok. I consider this Disney’s answer to Tubi, and we may see more of this soon-ish as Bob Iger seems eager to push out more content. He’s got that OpenAI deal locked, so what comes after nostalgia? Or maybe they are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8978dd06-cd97-41e7-8ae3-3f603a369b99?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">just copying</a><span> Paramount Pictures.</span></p>
<ol style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;list-style-type: lower-alpha;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">Rough Notes: This strategy seems to be born out of the desire to be one of those movies and TV shows that always gets clipped by engagement farms. The type of content that does rounds on TikTok is usually slop or ragebait. Shows like The Middle, Family Guy, and The Rookie have a factor to them to have some pretty bland or provocative episodes. Entertainment brands have to factor that in before going big with this strategy.</p></li></ol>
</li>
</ol>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="706"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/68cd1ce1-241f-4576-9cea-cd4d0d8e8e68?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="Loewe New Year Lantern Festival Activation, Yuyuan Garden Nanjing China." data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/ebddfb1d-3b22-4416-867f-83b185916f2e\_1880x1060.webp","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":821,"width":1456,"resizeWidth":706,"bytes":null,"alt":"Loewe New Year Lantern Festival Activation, Yuyuan Garden Nanjing China.","title":null,"type":null,"href":null,"belowTheFold":true,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":null,"isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="310.1304945054945" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!vRgh!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Febddfb1d-3b22-4416-867f-83b185916f2e\_1880x1060.webp" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" title="Loewe New Year Lantern Festival Activation, Yuyuan Garden Nanjing China." width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ol start="7" style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>I covered </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7ad94a7d-4f48-4bc9-b0c2-d4dd4ed4919c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Lunar New Year marketing campaigns</a><span> on Instagram, and this year’s campaigns are really good. Valentino gave strong competition to Loewe with their Lantern Festival. But overall, the energy of the campaigns doesn’t match the energy of the collections. This creator on TikTok reviewed </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0fa6cfb9-9b94-426a-9f7f-06c459e1f446?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a lot of the LNY collections</a><span>, and her reviews helped me a lot to connect the marketing with the actual product. The brand that kind of nailed both has to be Loewe, my favourite campaign overall is Panda Express.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><strong>The Mini-Trends &amp; Non-Trends…</strong></p>
<ol style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;list-style-type: lower-alpha;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">Super Bowl is pure celebrity culture, but we also got a ton of celebrity + influencer campaigns in other categories.</p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">Jacquemus announcing his grandmother as brand ambassador is going to kick off a generation of influencer-led and Instagram brands announcing their family and friends as ambassadors.</p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>This is my hot take of the week, video edits have gone too far. As much as I loved </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7f1b93d2-af2e-40bb-98d9-c5716604610d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">this video and format</a><span>, I’m like, video editing in 2026 is straight up gaslighting? You might have the worst experience at an event for God knows what reason. Then you see the video edit and it’s like, man, that sh\*t was cool.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>If your email marketing is good enough, maybe it will end on social media, that’s what happened to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e00635b2-8513-4f0e-945f-7cf295962273?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">reformation’s email</a><span> cracking </span><a href="https://substack.com/@vikkirosswrites/note/c-203518486?r=p750n&amp;utm\_medium=ios&amp;utm\_source=notes-share-action" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a joke</a><span> about Beckham family drama.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>IKEA’s flat-pack box OOH campaign, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4249e508-4b7e-48d7-93de-8dd7dfd53da7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">combined with</a><span> the context of their Oxford Street campaign from 2024, both made me think about why brands don’t prioritise brand assets over brand advertising when it comes to using scaffoldings as an OOH placement. Most scaffoldings stay for years or months. Instead of doing a quick promo, what if you wrap the building for a long period in your brand colours or a key brand asset? It’s better and less of an eyesore.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>After Raising Cane’s Fashion Week, brands in the USA are getting serious about fashion, in a more or less artistic way. </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/324996d9-67ae-463a-8335-53f563636772?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">NFL</a><span> is hosting a </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a681f7f5-e79e-41cc-89ac-d4d9882f9014?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Pre-Super Bowl Fashion Show</a><span>, sponsored by Abercrombie &amp; Fitch. Who needs approval from Paris or Milan? Brands in the U.S. are betting on </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0009314b-dc72-4efd-bc23-a82a3b5425c6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">sports culture</a><span> to win the masses, not the critics.</span></p>
<ol style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;list-style-type: lower-roman;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Thom Browne is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/582760af-beb9-4388-95b4-b2fe4afa3a38?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">also debuting</a><span> his Fall 2026 collection at the GQ Bowl in San Francisco. I’m not mad about it, even though I sound mad. It’s good business, and maybe this will make American football fans more fashionable, like the stars they follow.</span></p></li></ol>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;">Just a few months ago, I was hearing that tech’s minimalist era is over, and it’s all medieval from now on. Maybe it’s too early to call a fade a fade. The new OpenAI founders-led campaign says that trend is not going mainstream anytime this year.</p>
<ol style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;list-style-type: lower-roman;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;">The rest is marketing campaigns, scroll &amp;…</p></li></ol>
</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cb975e32-9f0a-4b3a-899e-623aedd36a63?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Best of Marketing recap</a><span> from last week and </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6af8d26f-acc9-444c-8b54-1fa5adb84fd7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">my favourite campaigns</a><span> from this week…</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Simon Porte Jacquemus’ </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/24c80bdf-1174-4e9e-9ae7-df2fe22f2c23?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">first brand ambassador</a><span> is his grandmother</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/21db0e9e-ae1b-4992-8c7c-84b09e8c3828?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> Liline Jacquemus</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Figs campaign </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9154a264-fa4b-4d5e-9d02-93b6e48df95f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">celebrates</a><span> the unsung heroes </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4caa700b-893a-4698-823f-a07610bd8fd5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">behind</a><span> sporting glory.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Figs scrubs just donated </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6911c1ae-5b91-4b19-a723-e0f1a02d67e0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">$25000</a><span> to Alex Pretti’s GoFundMe.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5b72137f-1dc3-4391-8833-39dbb7a4c110?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Blackpink’s LISA </a><span>debuts Kim K and Nike’s latest </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/28beb712-b9a7-41f6-9253-4621f6fbff85?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">NikeSKIMS Rift Satin</a><span> colorway.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Starbucks stresses </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/18ffdd41-d387-4d20-9a43-28d0bed4db77?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">togetherness</a><span> in new ad campaign.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ca4cf27c-0bbe-4d2b-9045-427291d89d53?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Dr Pepper</a><span> turns viral creator TikTok jingle into a CFP National Championship ad—</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d0d8de9b-71fd-46ff-8701-848b6ab559f3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">behind the strategy.</a></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>People are trying to</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/de03135e-7a50-42f1-bdbc-a2c07cdac457?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> make their own soda jingles</a><span> after the Dr. Pepper success—some are better than others.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Target, General Mills and 3M among </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/840ced64-53e4-4040-93f4-82f432eb98ec?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Minnesota brands</a><span> calling for ‘de-escalation’ amid ICE tensions.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5df46466-e19b-4050-9fe8-847dc6f75b4d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Crocs</a><span> marks next chapter with ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/41330ff1-aad9-420f-9ad5-0234bac40d21?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Wonderfully Unordinary</a><span>’ global campaign.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>In new OOH campaign, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/418de27a-c6a4-456a-b0f8-82c451cd50c7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Stella Artois</a><span> visually turns </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cdec1977-ed80-4ff9-8895-d312889d0e74?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Toronto’s heavy snow</a><span> into </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a33b15b3-0412-4ad9-9030-305ab10f82dc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">beer foam</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Heinz UAE launches “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/761e9892-d50e-4f0a-afde-56871a5a8768?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Never Enough</a><span>” campaign, part of “It has to be Heinz” platform. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Coke’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1e3bf977-2481-40ea-9706-ea84d8d527a7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">World Cup campaign</a><span> taps into unifying power of fan emotions.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/afe58dfb-9663-47ae-b0ea-f61408588a04?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Aldi Ireland</a><span> deploys OOH </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d829fb33-2e7e-4630-8fbe-0b82e7bc8fc9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">across</a><span> Dublin and Cork as part of </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/95aa992e-4b61-4b47-9ea7-5a3eaf0e26b4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">It’s Not Complicated push</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/74949bc5-ce5d-4767-a697-53083fbf2486?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Jeep</a><span> doubles down on adventure in “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0651e542-e0e5-44b1-8dd8-d9472080d707?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Jeep Things</a><span>” 85th anniversary campaign.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Panda Express celebrates </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0e64dc27-933f-4d60-9505-995438ea0877?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Lunar New Year</a><span> with handcrafted animated short.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e716961b-cf52-4661-ba40-f7e09b1fc105?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Yes</a><span>, those are real ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a3a69e45-d109-462c-891d-b6fc0a33beaf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Oreo Cows</a><span>’ in Oreo’s new milk-dunking commercial.</span></p></li>
</ul>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a8585ba6-cbb2-49fa-ad98-d843e56f814c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Build-A-Bear</a><span> takes us to first day at a new school in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a5d533c0-e150-476b-903a-90c1efdd1369?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ad spot</a><span> from Highdive.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Clorox to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0af43c71-3c09-4cdc-a554-225fdd39862f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">buy Purell-maker Gojo</a><span> for $2.25 billion.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Moynat brought onboard </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b40742ce-c2de-4c35-954a-63f213d3ed3c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Lucy Liu</a><span>, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/69ffd01e-51c7-4628-9b99-dcfa467ea4e9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Angela Bassett</a><span>, Martha Stewart, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/07d88199-a2f2-4729-a221-dc765561ec1f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Jordan Clarkson</a><span>, and many more celebs for their collaboration with Kasing Lung.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Everyone is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7fe434fc-9faa-4cb3-9a7e-baddf2d7e096?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">talking</a><span> about the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/72831742-87bb-40f6-9623-9cbefbea5705?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">UK Green Party’s latest video campaign</a><span>, and it’s worth your attention.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>MAA Ad of the Week: </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b403fe46-9c8f-4938-99ef-c6af2873f096?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Harvey Nichols</a><span> by adam&amp;eveDDB – from 2016.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/815564df-d69e-404e-89e5-7b4bed3bf2cd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Apple</a><span> debuts its</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/635ec2df-a6fe-4bfe-8120-bcdf777c6d28?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> 2026 Chinese New Year short film</a><span> Shot on iPhone 17 Pro.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Spotify’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c0475b4d-83e4-405b-af57-0759be2069af?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">OOH campaign</a><span> shows Lola Young, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/80b802c3-366e-4f61-85a5-dc5c40b3aa21?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Olivia Dean</a><span> and Addison Rae </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3a961bb9-da36-4455-8917-e10e9f4be020?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">before</a><span> fame.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Mozilla’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bb71f34e-8329-47b9-ad6d-1caa81f8c539?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">surreal new campaign</a><span> urges humanity to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ed835a71-85f9-421f-a5cf-e294ce2f38ee?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">take back</a><span> control.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>New gameshow by </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/89cb8937-6740-4ef5-88d5-3c366501d71f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Childline</a><span> mirrors the experience of digital anxiety.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>German street magicians </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e57f41cd-d879-4eac-99c8-03330ea71f97?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Siegfried &amp; Joy</a><span> (see what they did there?) become office magicians in a </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b0c6eda7-b927-42d3-967b-113a5ac18514?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Canva commercial</a><span> that goes for gold.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Novo Nordisk aims to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/53dbf464-ef05-4fdf-b6aa-67f8f0f62d2c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">set Ozempic apart</a><span> from other GLP-1s in new ad campaign.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/44023d54-6434-4c1f-b311-2c7b9033772c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Wingstop UK</a><span> adds new corn to the menu with a surrealist influencer-led campaign. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>We tried the viral </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7bd39c38-56c1-483b-8f99-3461858c98ae?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Progresso Soup drops</a><span> &amp; have strong feelings.</span></p></li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Taco Bell’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7d589f32-e910-4b92-b88f-d394a90df57c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">new platform</a><span> turns its latest value menu into high-end luxury.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5cd09a89-41fb-47a5-ad6f-0b445fa6696f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Hugh Jackman</a><span> wakes up with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c97e6967-b9e4-4af0-a248-2c95d6589256?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AG1</a><span> in new global campaign.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bc1b13e3-c8e3-496d-8b06-8231b751d0dc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Text on the Beach</a><span>’: California Post debuts with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/25089c82-b6d5-45ad-a756-6500f3f027b1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">backlash</a><span>, pun-filled brand campaign</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/09831884-002e-410f-8435-ab6f14b66f0b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Voy men’s hormones</a><span> ads prompt 33 complaints to ASA.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Coinbase musical ad by Mother gets </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7240e883-bcd0-4cd0-8f8f-0e207d4ab212?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ASA ban</a><span>.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Koto evolves </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d7ad880a-c40c-4d1c-a5c9-2a72e852e1a4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">GoFundMe’s brand</a><span> as platform expands beyond individual giving.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Whitefish Credit Union entered Missoula with</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/49f86758-a9ca-4e87-916e-842ee771366f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> billboards that didn’t look like</a><span> financial advertising at all.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Timbaland collabs with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e7193572-e710-4b1b-9d45-a2a3d2225a17?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Babybel</a><span> to produce “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e218fbfb-65c0-47a8-8cfc-ef2cabd414ff?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Mini Wax Tracks</a><span>.”</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The BBC marks the upcoming finale of its game show </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/72ee086a-0931-4be3-b310-85843e9b183c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">The Traitors</a><span> with a projection across the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/748ed459-89d9-4412-9cd8-bf9d3e31e9ca?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">White Cliffs of Dover</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The FCA calls in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f0cfdd20-57fa-4f66-bc4e-f3274f57542e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Emil the Seal</a><span> to stop consumers slipping into scams.</span></p></li>

Your Way</a><span>.”</span></p></li>

</li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fd9cfde9-d129-4ce7-84c8-239b8fbc2947?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Everyone</a><span> is talking about Team Mongolia’s ceremonial outfits for the Milan Cortina 2026 Winter Olympics, designed by Mongolian brand </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/926301c2-ade7-441c-aae6-8280a5907d66?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Goyol Cashmere</a><span>.</span></p></li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">

</li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/92bcbf3a-762a-41e1-a546-0ffa8a609a47?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Oakley</a><span> is bringing together Spike Lee and ISHOWSPEED for Super Bowl ad. </span></p></li>

</li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f8f93025-c50b-4357-93a7-a9e9f2960ad1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">LOEWE’s newest collection</a><span> is a dreamscape of cats and leather.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Bonsai producer and artisan Teppei Kojima stars in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/72b8dcb3-b181-47f8-84b5-7bb5c8a3b5fb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Jimmy Choo’s latest campaign</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>People are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7d4250b5-c8a3-4041-8e8d-27eca1b3d159?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">talking about</a><span> the world-building behind Paige Lorenz’s brand, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c59d2b11-9fe4-46d5-af3f-6992413f6e11?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Dairy Boy</a><span>, and </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3dd48a31-705e-4b75-8e64-e8a383e571dd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">the new collection rollout</a><span> was interesting.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Prada cuts ties with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6735ba42-7fb3-4600-a027-e23ed10ef097?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">over 200 suppliers</a><span> after labour abuse audit.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Kaia Gerber joins </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2a1f3d26-ed54-43fa-9fe2-15d6bf920e82?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Re/Done</a><span> as investor and ‘creative partner.’</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Bulgari taps </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/96d6335d-5694-4af7-b371-c757c1c2c6dd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Icon Isabella Rossellini</a><span> for launch of new Minaudières collection.</span></p></li>

Cosmetics.</span></p></li>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Quince </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d94b3e94-c1ec-479f-8f74-f36a09e63ef6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">collaborates</a><span> with A$AP Rocky on exclusive products for ‘Don’t be Dumb’ album release.</span></p></li></ul>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>‘Tell Me Lies’ Stars Grace Van Patten and Jackson White font Calvin Klein’s ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9f5e223b-9052-4350-9de0-4177ae869ec2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Our Calvins</a><span>’ Valentine’s Day campaign.</span></p></li>

</li>
